(herald "Verification of TLS 1.3 rules to assist in mTLS verification"
  (try-old-strands))

(defprotocol mtls13 basic
  (defrole client
    (vars (s c ca name) (cr sr random32) (x rndx) (y expt)
      (spk cpk akey) (request response text))
    (trace (send (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (recv
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                  spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk)))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (send
        (enc (cat c cpk (enc (hash c cpk) (privk ca)))
          (enc
            (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                  spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))
              (hash
                (hash "finished"
                  (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
                (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
                (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
                (enc
                  (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                    spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))) c
              cpk (enc (hash c cpk) (privk ca))) (invk cpk))
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                  spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk)))
            (cat c cpk (enc (hash c cpk) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
                (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
                (enc
                  (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                    spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))
                (hash
                  (hash "finished"
                    (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
                  (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
                  (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
                  (enc
                    (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
                      s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk)))
                c cpk (enc (hash c cpk) (privk ca))) (invk cpk)))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (send
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived"))))
      (recv
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived")))))
    (uniq-gen x))
  (defrole server
    (vars (s c ca name) (cr sr random32) (y rndx) (x expt)
      (spk cpk akey) (request response text))
    (trace (recv (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (send
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                  spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk)))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (recv
        (enc (cat c cpk (enc (hash c cpk) (privk ca)))
          (enc
            (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                  spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))
              (hash
                (hash "finished"
                  (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
                (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
                (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
                (enc
                  (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                    spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))) c
              cpk (enc (hash c cpk) (privk ca))) (invk cpk))
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                  spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk)))
            (cat c cpk (enc (hash c cpk) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
                (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
                (enc
                  (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                    spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))
                (hash
                  (hash "finished"
                    (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
                  (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
                  (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
                  (enc
                    (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
                      s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk)))
                c cpk (enc (hash c cpk) (privk ca))) (invk cpk)))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (recv
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "derived"))))
      (send
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "derived")))))
    (uniq-gen y)
    (absent (y x)))
  (defrule clients-servers-distinct
    (forall ((z0 z1 strd) (c name))
      (implies
        (and (p "client" "s" z1 c) (p "client" "c" z0 c))
        (false))))
  (defrule client-exponent-unavailable
    (forall ((x rndx) (z z-0 strd))
      (implies
        (and (p "" z-0 (idx 2)) (p "client" z (idx 1)) (p "" "x" z-0 x)
          (p "client" "x" z x) (ugen x))
        (false))))
  (defrule server-exponent-unavailable
    (forall ((y rndx) (z z-0 strd))
      (implies
        (and (p "" z-0 (idx 2)) (p "server" z (idx 2)) (p "" "x" z-0 y)
          (p "server" "y" z y) (ugen y))
        (false))))
  (defrule diffie-hellman-value-between-legitimate-parties-unavailable-1
    (forall
      ((cr random32) (spk akey) (s ca name) (x rndx) (y expt)
        (z z-0 strd))
      (implies
        (and (p "" z-0 (idx 2)) (p "client" z (idx 2))
          (p "client" "spk" z spk) (p "client" "ca" z ca)
          (p "client" "s" z s) (p "" "x" z-0 (exp (gen) (mul x y)))
          (p "client" "y" z y) (p "client" "x" z x)
          (p "client" "cr" z cr) (non (invk spk)) (uniq-at cr z (idx 0))
          (ugen x))
        (false))))
  (defrule diffie-hellman-value-between-legitimate-parties-unavailable-2
    (forall
      ((sr random32) (cpk akey) (c ca name) (y rndx) (x expt)
        (z z-0 strd))
      (implies
        (and (p "" z-0 (idx 2)) (p "server" z (idx 3))
          (p "server" "cpk" z cpk) (p "server" "ca" z ca)
          (p "server" "c" z c) (p "" "x" z-0 (exp (gen) (mul y x)))
          (p "server" "y" z y) (p "server" "x" z x)
          (p "server" "sr" z sr) (non (invk cpk)) (uniq-at sr z (idx 1))
          (ugen y))
        (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_indx
    (forall ((x indx)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_strd
    (forall ((x strd)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_mesg
    (forall ((x mesg)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (lang (random32 atom)))

(defskeleton mtls13
  (vars (cr sr cr-0 sr-0 cr-1 sr-1 random32)
    (request response request-0 request-1 text)
    (spk cpk spk-0 cpk-0 spk-1 cpk-1 akey)
    (s c ca s-0 c-0 ca-0 s-1 c-1 ca-1 name) (w rndx) (x expt) (y rndx)
    (y-0 y-1 expt))
  (defstrand server 5 (cr cr) (sr sr) (request request)
    (response response) (spk spk) (cpk cpk) (s s) (c c) (ca ca) (y y)
    (x x))
  (defstrand client 4 (cr cr-0) (sr sr-0) (request request-0)
    (spk spk-0) (cpk cpk-0) (s s-0) (c c-0) (ca ca-0) (x w) (y y-0))
  (defstrand client 4 (cr cr-1) (sr sr-1) (request request-1)
    (spk spk-1) (cpk cpk-1) (s s-1) (c c-1) (ca ca-1) (x w) (y y-1))
  (non-orig (invk cpk))
  (uniq-orig sr)
  (uniq-gen w y)
  (absent (y x))
  (goals
    (forall
      ((cr sr random32) (spk cpk akey) (w rndx) (x expt) (y rndx)
        (z z1 z2 strd))
      (implies
        (and (p "server" z 5) (p "server" "cr" z cr)
          (p "server" "sr" z sr) (p "server" "x" z x)
          (p "server" "y" z y) (p "server" "spk" z spk)
          (p "server" "cpk" z cpk) (p "client" z1 4)
          (p "client" "x" z1 w) (p "client" z2 4) (p "client" "x" z2 w)
          (uniq-at sr z 1) (ugen y) (non (invk cpk))) (= z1 z2))))
  (traces
    ((recv (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (send
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (enc

      (recv

          (enc
            (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                  spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))
              (hash
                (hash "finished"
                  (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
                (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
                (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
                (enc
                  (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                    spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))) c
              cpk (enc (hash c cpk) (privk ca))) (invk cpk))
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))
            (hash

              (enc

            (cat c cpk (enc (hash c cpk) (privk ca)))
            (enc
